Same Outcome, Different Evidence: Intent Recovery in LLM Safety Evaluation
Authors: Haitong Jiang, Chunlin Liu, Sihan Tang, Chan Wu, Xiaoqing Su, Yuhong Feng
First: 2026-10-08T11:50:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-08T11:50:32+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 2 figures. Code and experiment inputs: https://github.com/kevinjiang0121-cyber/IRIS
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Safety evaluations of large language models commonly summarize harmful-output behavior with attack success rate (ASR). Yet the same non-harmful outcome can arise for very different reasons. A model may recover a harmful task and refuse it, fail to recover the task, or respond to something else entirely. Distinguishing these cases becomes especially important under intent-obscuring prompts, where a low ASR does not reveal whether the evaluated task was actually engaged. To make this distinction explicit, we pair ASR with operative understanding rate (UR), which measures whether a response both identifies the evaluated task and treats it as the task to be answered. Across interfaces, this paired view reveals substantial variation hidden by ASR: similar ASR values can correspond to sharply different recovery rates. Controlled English reconstructions show that recovery consistently improves as compressed prompts become more explicit, whereas ASR does not follow the same pattern. A complementary contrast comes from FormalLogic, where high recovery can still coincide with frequent harmful assistance. Together, these results show that non-harmful outcomes are not equally informative about model safety, motivating the joint reporting of intent recovery and ASR in LLM safety evaluation. Code and experiment inputs are available at https://github.com/kevinjiang0121-cyber/IRIS.
中文标题/摘要
标题：相同结果，不同证据：大型语言模型安全评估中的意图恢复
大型语言模型的安全评估通常通过攻击成功率（ASR）来总结有害输出行为。然而，相同的非有害结果可能由非常不同的原因引起。模型可能恢复有害任务并拒绝它，未能恢复任务，或完全响应其他内容。在意图模糊的提示下，区分这些情况变得尤为重要，因为低ASR并不能揭示被评估的任务是否真正被参与。为了明确这一区分，我们将ASR与操作理解率（UR）配对，后者衡量响应是否同时识别被评估的任务并将其视为需要回答的任务。在不同接口中，这种配对视角揭示了ASR隐藏的显著变化：相似的ASR值可能对应于截然不同的恢复率。受控的英语重构表明，随着压缩提示变得更加明确，恢复率持续提高，而ASR并未遵循相同的模式。来自FormalLogic的补充对比显示，高恢复率仍可能与频繁的有害协助相吻合。综合这些结果表明，非有害结果并不对模型安全提供同等的信息，促使在LLM安全评估中联合报告意图恢复和ASR。代码和实验输入可在https://github.com/kevinjiang0121-cyber/IRIS获取。
Summary / 总结
This article addresses the limitations of current safety evaluations for large language models (LLMs), which typically rely on attack success rate (ASR) to summarize harmful-output behavior. Previous methods fail to differentiate between various reasons for non-harmful outcomes, leading to potential misinterpretations of model safety. The proposed approach introduces a dual metric system that combines ASR with an operative understanding rate (UR), allowing for a clearer distinction of intent recovery in response to prompts. This methodology reveals that similar ASR values can mask significant differences in recovery rates, with findings indicating that more explicit prompts lead to better recovery outcomes. The study demonstrates that non-harmful outcomes do not uniformly indicate model safety, advocating for the joint reporting of intent recovery and ASR in LLM evaluations, thus contributing to a more nuanced understanding of model behavior.
本研究解决了当前大型语言模型（LLM）安全评估的局限性，现有评估通常依赖攻击成功率（ASR）来总结有害输出行为。以往的方法未能区分非有害结果的不同原因，可能导致对模型安全性的误解。本文提出了一种新方法，将ASR与操作理解率（UR）结合，以提供模型意图恢复能力的更清晰图景。该方法论涉及控制的英语重构和使用FormalLogic分析不同提示条件下的恢复率。研究结果表明，尽管ASR可能保持不变，但恢复率可能显著不同，这表明非有害结果并不均匀地指示模型安全性。这一发现促使在LLM安全评估中需要联合报告意图恢复和ASR，从而增强对模型行为的理解。
Pretext: Defeating Malicious Skill Detection Frameworks for AI Agents
Authors: Tobias Kaiser, Aritra Dhar
Venue: NeurIPS 2026
First: 2026-09-30T12:26:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-08T08:23:49+00:00
Comments: Accepted in AIWild@NeurIPS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Skills extend an agent's capabilities by injecting instructions and information into the context, and are widely used by agents such as OpenClaw and Claude Code. Prior work shows third-party marketplaces host malicious skills that give attackers direct influence over the victim's agent. The emerging defense scans skills before installation, pairing deterministic static checks with an LLM-based semantic judge, as in NVIDIA's SkillSpector. We show that such defenses fall to an attacker who knows the detector. Our white-box LLM attacker, Pretext, iteratively crafts skills that evade detection while still delivering the payload and performing the benign task: moving the payload from code into natural language leaves static analysis inert, while framing it as the skill's legitimate purpose and splitting instructions across files keeps the LLM stage below its blocking threshold. Across three open-source models, Pretext achieves up to 97\% and 77\% against a frozen detector and a co-adaptive one, respectively, revealing major gaps in current skill scanners.
中文标题/摘要
标题：前提：击败恶意技能检测框架的AI代理
技能通过将指令和信息注入上下文来扩展代理的能力，广泛应用于OpenClaw和Claude Code等代理。先前的研究表明，第三方市场上存在恶意技能，使攻击者能够直接影响受害者的代理。新兴的防御措施在安装前扫描技能，将确定性静态检查与基于LLM的语义判断相结合，如NVIDIA的SkillSpector。我们表明，这种防御措施会被了解检测器的攻击者攻破。我们的白盒LLM攻击者Pretext迭代地构造能够规避检测的技能，同时仍然传递有效负载并执行良性任务：将有效负载从代码转移到自然语言使静态分析失效，而将其框架设为技能的合法目的并将指令分散到多个文件中则使LLM阶段保持在其阻断阈值以下。在三个开源模型中，Pretext在冻结检测器和共适应检测器上分别达到了97%和77%的成功率，揭示了当前技能扫描器的重大缺陷。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the vulnerability of AI agents to malicious skills that can be injected through third-party marketplaces, which can compromise the integrity of the agents like OpenClaw and Claude Code. Previous methods, such as NVIDIA's SkillSpector, utilize static checks combined with LLM-based semantic judges to detect these malicious skills; however, they are ineffective against attackers who understand the detection mechanisms. The proposed approach, Pretext, is a white-box LLM attacker that crafts skills to evade detection while still executing malicious tasks, effectively bypassing static analysis by disguising harmful payloads as legitimate instructions. This paper contributes to the field by demonstrating significant weaknesses in existing skill detection frameworks, achieving detection evasion rates of up to 97% against static detectors and 77% against adaptive ones across three open-source models, thus highlighting the need for improved defenses in AI skill management.
本研究探讨了AI代理在第三方市场中注入恶意技能的脆弱性，这对代理构成了重大威胁，因为攻击者可以操控这些代理。以往的方法，如NVIDIA的SkillSpector，结合静态检查和基于LLM的语义分析来检测这些恶意技能，但对于了解检测机制的攻击者而言，这些方法并不有效。所提出的方法Pretext创新性地构建了能够规避检测的技能，利用对检测框架的理解，保持有效负载的功能，同时将其伪装为合法内容。该方法的提出是有充分动机的，因为它突显了现有防御的不足。本文的贡献在于展示Pretext在三个开源模型中对静态检测器的规避率高达97%，对共适应检测器的规避率为77%，强调了当前技能扫描技术的重大缺陷。
TACS: Trajectory-Aware Candidate Selection for LLM Jailbreak Suffix Optimization
Authors: Shiliang Xiao
First: 2026-08-30T05:18:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-07T16:21:07+00:00
Comments: We identified an error in the theoretical analysis, which affects the validity of the main conclusions of the manuscript. Since the current version does not adequately support this conclusion, we have decided to withdraw the paper
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Gradient-based jailbreak suffix optimization methods typically update the suffix by retaining the candidate with the lowest current loss. We show that this seemingly natural design is fundamentally myopic: candidates that look better under the current-step proxy often fail to produce better jailbreak outcomes later in the search, revealing a form of selection-stage reward hacking. This suggests that candidate selection, rather than candidate generation alone, is a hidden bottleneck in suffix optimization. To address this issue, we propose TACS, a trajectory-aware candidate selection framework for jailbreak suffix optimization. Instead of selecting candidates solely by their immediate loss, TACS augments per-step evaluation with a trajectory-aware proxy and stabilizes selection with reference-policy regularization and a discriminator-estimated chi-squared correction, encouraging choices that remain effective beyond the current step. Experiments on HarmBench show that TACS consistently outperforms strong baselines under the same search budget, substantially improving attack success rates while exhibiting more stable optimization behavior throughout the search. Our findings highlight that mitigating selection-stage reward hacking caused by myopic candidate selection is critical for improving jailbreak suffix optimization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TACS：基于轨迹的LLM越狱后缀优化候选选择
基于梯度的越狱后缀优化方法通常通过保留当前损失最低的候选来更新后缀。我们表明，这种看似自然的设计在根本上是短视的：在当前步骤代理下看起来更好的候选往往在搜索后期未能产生更好的越狱结果，揭示了一种选择阶段奖励黑客行为。这表明候选选择，而不仅仅是候选生成，是后缀优化中的一个隐藏瓶颈。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了TACS，一个用于越狱后缀优化的基于轨迹的候选选择框架。TACS不仅仅通过候选的即时损失来选择候选，而是通过轨迹感知代理增强每一步的评估，并通过参考策略正则化和判别器估计的卡方修正来稳定选择，鼓励在当前步骤之外仍然有效的选择。在HarmBench上的实验表明，TACS在相同搜索预算下始终优于强基线，显著提高了攻击成功率，同时在整个搜索过程中表现出更稳定的优化行为。我们的研究结果强调，减轻由短视候选选择引起的选择阶段奖励黑客行为对于改善越狱后缀优化至关重要。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the limitations of existing gradient-based jailbreak suffix optimization methods, which typically select candidates based solely on the lowest current loss, leading to myopic decision-making and suboptimal outcomes. The proposed method, TACS, introduces a trajectory-aware candidate selection framework that enhances the evaluation process by incorporating a trajectory-aware proxy and stabilizing selection through reference-policy regularization and a chi-squared correction. This approach effectively mitigates the issues of selection-stage reward hacking and improves the overall optimization process. The methodology was tested on HarmBench, demonstrating that TACS significantly outperforms traditional methods under the same search budget, achieving higher attack success rates and more stable optimization behavior, thereby supporting the goal of enhancing jailbreak suffix optimization.
本研究解决了现有基于梯度的越狱后缀优化方法的局限性，这些方法通常仅依赖于根据即时损失选择候选者，导致由于选择阶段奖励黑客行为而产生的次优长期结果。提出的TACS框架通过引入一种轨迹感知的候选选择过程来改进这些方法，该过程不仅根据当前表现评估候选者，还考虑其在未来步骤中的潜在有效性，利用参考策略正则化和卡方修正来提高稳定性。本文的贡献在于证明解决候选选择的隐性瓶颈可以显著增强优化结果。在HarmBench上进行的实验表明，TACS在攻击成功率和优化行为稳定性方面均优于现有基线，从而支持改善越狱后缀优化的目标。
PatchBench: Measuring Collateral Damage in Activation Patching
Authors: Alexi Canesse, Mathis Le Bail, Maël Jenny, Clément Elliker, Mahammed El Sharkawy, Sonia Vanier
Venue: NeurIPS 2026
First: 2026-10-07T15:43:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-07T15:43:28+00:00
Comments: Accepted to NeurIPS 2026 (Datasets and Benchmarks Track)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
An LLM safety patch can pass a benchmark while still being a poor repair. This risk is especially acute for jailbreak repairs, where the goal is to correct a specific unsafe behaviour without changing unrelated behaviours. A patch may block exact evaluation prompts yet fail on close harmful variants, or suppress harmful behaviour by over-refusing benign prompts that share its wording or structure. Existing protocols primarily test whether models can be broken, while aggregate metrics (attack success, refusal rates, global capability) cannot distinguish selective repairs from broader local suppression. To address this gap, we introduce PatchBench, a benchmark of empirically observed model-specific jailbreak failures inducing actionable harmful answers. Starting from 27,870 prompts from 37 public datasets, we curate 15,314 English prompts and query 8 open-source instruction-tuned models. Combining WildGuard filtering, pairwise Elo ranking, and manual verification, we retain a curated bank of 400 high-confidence jailbreak failures. We further introduce PatchBench-Local, an evaluation protocol testing whether a patch is behaviourally precise. For each harmful source prompt, PatchBench-Local generates three families of local neighbours: harmful variants preserving malicious intent, benign prompts with matched structure, and benign prompts reusing key harmful terms. It evaluates harmful-neighbour correction and benign-neighbour preservation, distinguishing selective repair from broader local suppression. Evaluating four activation steering methods with PatchBench-Local and MMLU shows that global capability can remain nearly unchanged while local benign regressions are severe, confirming aggregate metrics miss important collateral damage. PatchBench-Local provides a more precise basis for developing and comparing jailbreak repair methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PatchBench：测量激活修补中的附带损害
LLM安全修补可以通过基准测试，但仍然是一个糟糕的修复。这种风险在越狱修复中尤为严重，其目标是纠正特定的不安全行为，而不改变无关的行为。一个修补可能会阻止精确的评估提示，但在接近的有害变体上失败，或者通过过度拒绝共享其措辞或结构的良性提示来抑制有害行为。现有协议主要测试模型是否可以被破坏，而聚合指标（攻击成功率、拒绝率、全球能力）无法区分选择性修复与更广泛的局部抑制。为了解决这一差距，我们引入了PatchBench，这是一个基于经验观察的模型特定越狱失败的基准，诱导可操作的有害答案。从37个公共数据集中提取的27,870个提示中，我们策划了15,314个英语提示，并查询了8个开源指令调优模型。结合WildGuard过滤、成对Elo排名和手动验证，我们保留了400个高置信度的越狱失败的策划库。我们进一步引入了PatchBench-Local，一种评估协议，测试修补是否在行为上精确。对于每个有害源提示，PatchBench-Local生成三类局部邻居：保留恶意意图的有害变体、结构匹配的良性提示和重用关键有害术语的良性提示。它评估有害邻居的修正和良性邻居的保留，区分选择性修复与更广泛的局部抑制。使用PatchBench-Local和MMLU评估四种激活引导方法表明，全球能力几乎可以保持不变，而局部良性回归却很严重，确认聚合指标错过了重要的附带损害。PatchBench-Local为开发和比较越狱修复方法提供了更精确的基础。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the inadequacies of existing benchmarks in evaluating the effectiveness of safety patches for large language models (LLMs), particularly in the context of jailbreak repairs that aim to correct specific unsafe behaviors without altering unrelated ones. Previous methods primarily focused on whether models could be compromised, failing to differentiate between selective repairs and broader local suppression, which can lead to misleading aggregate metrics. The proposed approach, PatchBench, introduces a benchmark that empirically measures model-specific jailbreak failures and evaluates the precision of patches through a novel evaluation protocol, PatchBench-Local. This methodology involves generating harmful and benign prompt variants to assess the effectiveness of patches in correcting harmful behaviors while preserving benign interactions. The findings reveal that while global model capabilities may remain stable, significant local regressions can occur, underscoring the importance of precise evaluation in the development of jailbreak repair methods.
本研究解决了现有基准在评估大型语言模型（LLMs）安全补丁有效性方面的局限性，特别是在旨在纠正特定不安全行为而不影响无关行为的越狱修复中。以往的方法主要关注模型是否会被破坏，未能区分选择性修复和更广泛的局部抑制，导致对补丁有效性的潜在误解。所提出的方法PatchBench引入了一个新的基准，实证测量模型特定的越狱失败，并通过一种新评估协议PatchBench-Local评估补丁的精确性。该方法涉及策划提示数据集，并评估补丁纠正有害行为的能力，同时保留良性响应。研究结果表明，尽管模型的整体能力可能保持稳定，但可能会出现显著的局部回归，表明传统的汇总指标可能忽视关键的附带损害，从而为评估越狱修复方法提供了更准确的框架。
Dynamic Budget Allocation for LLM Evaluation under Hard Resource Constraints
Authors: Shai Feldman, Yaniv Romano
First: 2026-10-05T20:31:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-05T20:31:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We evaluate large language models (LLMs) in multi-turn interactions through their time-to-event: the number of interaction steps required to produce an event of interest, such as a successful jailbreak or agentic task completion. Under limited compute, interactions may be terminated before the event occurs, so that event times are only partially observed (censored). Existing allocation methods for calibrating time-to-event bounds satisfy the budget only in expectation and can exceed the available budget on a particular evaluation run. Enforcing a hard constraint is particularly challenging as the cost of a trajectory is initially unknown. We introduce Hard-budget Allocation with Reflow for Predictive calibration (HARP), a budget allocation that satisfies hard resource constraints and adaptively reallocates unused budget. We show how to use HARP to construct lower predictive bounds (LPBs) on the time-to-event and to estimate evaluation metrics such as the jailbreak rate on a fixed benchmark. Although HARP induces dependence in acquisition decisions across different trajectories, we prove that HARP never exceeds the target budget, that its LPBs have finite-sample coverage guarantees, and that its metric estimates are unbiased. Experiments on agentic task success, LLM jailbreaks, toxic content generation, and RAG hallucinations show that HARP achieves coverage close to the nominal level with low variance, while never exceeding the given budget.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在严格资源限制下的LLM评估动态预算分配
我们通过事件发生的时间来评估大型语言模型（LLMs）在多轮交互中的表现：产生感兴趣事件所需的交互步骤数，例如成功越狱或代理任务完成。在计算资源有限的情况下，交互可能在事件发生之前被终止，因此事件时间仅部分可观察（被审查）。现有的时间到事件界限的校准分配方法仅在期望上满足预算，并且在特定评估运行中可能超过可用预算。强制执行硬约束尤其具有挑战性，因为轨迹的成本最初是未知的。我们引入了用于预测校准的硬预算分配与再分配（HARP），这是一种满足严格资源约束并自适应重新分配未使用预算的预算分配方法。我们展示了如何使用HARP构建时间到事件的下限预测界限（LPBs），并估计固定基准上的评估指标，如越狱率。尽管HARP在不同轨迹之间的获取决策中引入了依赖性，但我们证明HARP从未超过目标预算，其LPBs具有有限样本覆盖保证，并且其指标估计是无偏的。在代理任务成功、LLM越狱、有毒内容生成和RAG幻觉的实验中，HARP在低方差的情况下实现了接近名义水平的覆盖，同时从未超过给定预算。
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the challenge of evaluating large language models (LLMs) in multi-turn interactions, particularly under hard resource constraints where interactions may be prematurely terminated, leading to censored event times. Previous methods for budget allocation in time-to-event evaluations only meet expectations and can exceed available resources, creating difficulties in enforcing strict constraints. The proposed Hard-budget Allocation with Reflow for Predictive calibration (HARP) method overcomes these limitations by ensuring that the budget is strictly adhered to while adaptively reallocating any unused resources. HARP contributes to the field by providing lower predictive bounds on time-to-event metrics and unbiased estimates for evaluation metrics, demonstrating its effectiveness through experiments on various tasks such as agentic task success and LLM jailbreaks, achieving reliable coverage without exceeding the budget.
本研究解决了在硬资源限制下评估大型语言模型（LLMs）在多轮交互中的挑战，特别是在交互可能被提前终止的情况下，导致事件时间被截断的问题。以往的时间到事件评估中的预算分配方法往往只能满足预算预期，并可能超出可用资源，这在实际应用中带来了重大问题。所提出的硬预算分配与重分配预测校准（HARP）方法克服了这些限制，确保严格遵守预算，并在未使用预算的情况下进行自适应重新分配。HARP通过提供时间到事件指标的下限预测界限和评估指标的无偏估计，为该领域做出了贡献，通过在代理任务成功和LLM越狱等多种任务上的实验，证明了其有效性，可靠地实现了覆盖并在整个评估过程中保持预算约束。
Local Sparsity Enables Unsupervised LLM Safety Detection
Authors: Xin Chen, Gil Kur, Alexander Shevchenko, Andreas Krause
First: 2026-09-17T12:23:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-05T18:05:40+00:00
Comments: Published at NeurIPS2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deployment-time safety methods for large language models (LLMs) are predominantly supervised and assume access to unsafe training data. Nevertheless, new attacks and harm categories regularly arise, not captured by models trained in such a supervised fashion. An alternative approach is to view this problem through the lens of anomaly detection, namely, to rely solely on modeling safe data and flagging out-of-distribution inputs. However, LLM activations lie in a high-dimensional space, raising concerns about whether anomaly detection is statistically feasible. We show that, under the linear representation hypothesis (LRH), there may indeed be hope. In the LRH concept space, which is typically recovered via a sparse autoencoder (SAE), nearby points share a small common active support. Using this local sparsity insight, we propose a framework for locally masked SAE-based anomaly detection, supported by theoretical justifications. We validate it on various architectures and datasets, including both capability-testing datasets and safety-specific datasets. Finally, when we allow algorithms to use 1% out-of-distribution data for calibration, locally sparse methods achieve near-optimal performance, demonstrating their ability to capture meaningful safety information while using only 1-2% of SAE neurons for computation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：局部稀疏性使无监督LLM安全检测成为可能
大型语言模型（LLMs）的部署时安全方法主要是监督性的，并假设可以访问不安全的训练数据。然而，新攻击和伤害类别定期出现，这些在以这种监督方式训练的模型中并未被捕获。另一种方法是通过异常检测的视角来看待这个问题，即仅依赖于建模安全数据并标记分布外输入。然而，LLM激活位于高维空间中，这引发了关于异常检测在统计上是否可行的担忧。我们表明，在线性表示假设（LRH）下，确实可能存在希望。在LRH概念空间中，通常通过稀疏自编码器（SAE）恢复，附近点共享一个小的共同活跃支持。利用这一局部稀疏性洞察，我们提出了一种基于局部掩蔽SAE的异常检测框架，并提供了理论依据。我们在各种架构和数据集上进行了验证，包括能力测试数据集和安全特定数据集。最后，当我们允许算法使用1%的分布外数据进行校准时，局部稀疏方法实现了接近最佳的性能，展示了它们在仅使用1-2%的SAE神经元进行计算时捕获有意义安全信息的能力。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the limitations of existing supervised safety methods for large language models (LLMs), which rely on unsafe training data and fail to adapt to new attacks and harm categories. Traditional approaches struggle with the high-dimensional nature of LLM activations, making anomaly detection challenging. This paper proposes a novel framework for locally masked sparse autoencoder (SAE)-based anomaly detection, leveraging the linear representation hypothesis (LRH) to model safe data and identify out-of-distribution inputs effectively. The methodology demonstrates that local sparsity can enhance detection performance, validated across various architectures and datasets, achieving near-optimal results with only 1-2% of SAE neurons, thus supporting the goal of improving LLM safety detection under limited data conditions.
本文解决了现有大型语言模型（LLMs）监督安全方法的局限性，这些方法依赖于不安全的训练数据，无法适应新出现的攻击类型。所提出的方法将重点转向异常检测，利用线性表示假设（LRH）来建模安全数据并识别分布外输入。该方法的动机在于观察到LRH概念空间中的邻近点共享少量公共活跃支持，从而有效检测异常。本文贡献了一种基于局部掩蔽稀疏自编码器（SAE）的异常检测框架，并在多种架构和数据集上进行了验证。结果表明，当仅用1%的分布外数据进行校准时，局部稀疏方法能够实现近乎最佳的性能，有效捕捉重要的安全信息，同时最小化计算资源的使用。
Agent MechSuits: Mechanistic Subspace Safety Steering for Multi-Turn CLI Agents
Authors: Weidi Luo, Qiming Zhang, Yihao Quan, Mingyu Jin, Jie Cai, Chaowei Xiao, Jingcheng Niu, Zhen Xiang
Venue: NeurIPS
First: 2026-06-21T21:13:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-05T17:21:29+00:00
Comments: Accepted by NeurIPS'2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Command-Line Interface (CLI) agents based on large language models (LLMs) demonstrate remarkable autonomous capabilities, but they also introduce significant safety and misuse risks during multi-turn interactions with external environments. Existing safety mechanisms mainly rely on external guardrails, which have a limited ability to perform fine-grained behavioral control during execution. Meanwhile, recent mechanistic interpretability methods for LLM safety are mostly confined to single-turn or jailbreak-style QA settings, limiting their ability to capture the evolving risk dynamics of multi-turn agent execution. In this paper, we investigate the safety of multi-turn CLI agents from an internal perspective. We propose Agent MechSuits (Mechanistic Subspace Intervention and Steering), a white-box defense framework that performs runtime safety detection and representation-level mitigation for CLI agents. Unlike conventional agent guardrails, Agent MechSuits detects harmful execution states from step-level hidden representations and mitigates unsafe behavior by intervening in a 10-dimensional subspace within a single layer. To support this research, we introduce the Mechanistic Agent Safety (MAS) benchmark, comprising comprehensively annotated multi-turn execution trajectories across 194 tasks using LLaMA-3.1-8B, Qwen-2.5-7B, and Gemma-2-9B. Extensive experiments show that Agent MechSuits achieves strong safety detection performance, provides preliminary evidence for lookahead risk anticipation, and substantially reduces harmful actions of the CLI agent, establishing a foundation for applying mechanistic interpretability to dynamic LLM agent safety.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代理机械套装：多轮CLI代理的机械子空间安全引导
基于大型语言模型（LLM）的命令行界面（CLI）代理展示了显著的自主能力，但在与外部环境的多轮交互中也引入了重大安全和误用风险。现有的安全机制主要依赖外部护栏，在执行过程中进行细粒度行为控制的能力有限。同时，最近的LLM安全机械解释方法大多局限于单轮或越狱式问答设置，限制了其捕捉多轮代理执行中不断变化的风险动态的能力。本文从内部视角研究多轮CLI代理的安全性。我们提出了代理机械套装（Agent MechSuits），这是一个白盒防御框架，能够对CLI代理进行运行时安全检测和表示级缓解。与传统的代理护栏不同，代理机械套装通过检测步骤级隐藏表示中的有害执行状态，并通过在单层内的10维子空间进行干预来缓解不安全行为。为了支持这项研究，我们引入了机械代理安全（MAS）基准，涵盖了使用LLaMA-3.1-8B、Qwen-2.5-7B和Gemma-2-9B的194个任务的全面注释的多轮执行轨迹。大量实验表明，代理机械套装在安全检测性能上表现出色，为前瞻性风险预判提供了初步证据，并显著减少了CLI代理的有害行为，为将机械解释应用于动态LLM代理安全奠定了基础。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the safety risks associated with multi-turn Command-Line Interface (CLI) agents powered by large language models (LLMs), which have shown impressive autonomous capabilities but also pose significant misuse risks. Previous safety mechanisms primarily relied on external guardrails, which lacked fine-grained control during execution, and existing interpretability methods were limited to single-turn scenarios, failing to capture the evolving risks in multi-turn interactions. The proposed approach, Agent MechSuits, introduces a white-box defense framework that enables runtime safety detection and mitigation by intervening in a 10-dimensional subspace of hidden representations, thus addressing the limitations of prior methods. This paper contributes by establishing the Mechanistic Agent Safety (MAS) benchmark, which includes annotated multi-turn execution trajectories across 194 tasks, and demonstrates that Agent MechSuits significantly enhances safety detection performance and reduces harmful actions in CLI agents, supporting the goal of improving agent safety in dynamic environments.
本研究关注由大型语言模型（LLM）驱动的多轮命令行界面（CLI）代理的安全性和误用风险，这些代理虽然展现出令人印象深刻的自主能力，但缺乏有效的安全机制。以往的方法主要依赖外部护栏，无法有效管理执行过程中的细粒度行为控制，而现有的可解释性方法仅限于单轮交互，未能考虑多轮上下文中的风险演变。提出的Agent MechSuits框架通过在表示层面进行运行时安全检测和干预，提供了一种新的内部视角，特别是通过在单层内的10维子空间针对有害执行状态进行干预。本文的贡献在于引入了机制代理安全（MAS）基准，包含194个任务的多轮执行数据注释，并展示了Agent MechSuits显著提高了安全检测能力并减少了有害行为，从而支持了提升动态LLM代理安全性的目标。
Beyond Refusal Patterns: Safe-Role Internalization for Robust and Generalizable LLM Safety Alignment
Authors: Jinghao Pang, Jitai Hao, Qiang Huang, Zhaochun Ren, Jun Yu
First: 2026-10-04T15:50:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-04T15:50:42+00:00
Comments: 27 pages,7 figures, under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable capabilities but remain vulnerable to jailbreak attacks that elicit harmful or unsafe outputs. Existing safety alignment approaches, including Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF), often require substantial attack-specific supervision and computational resources, while remaining susceptible to shallow safety alignment and over-refusal. To address these challenges, we introduce SSRFT(Supervised Safe-Role Fine-Tuning), the first framework that reformulates safety alignment as the internalization of a predefined safe role. SSRFT constructs a Safe-Role Question-Answer (SRQA) dataset from psychometric questions, limited jailbreak prompts, and a safe-role description. Role-consistent responses are synthesized, validated, and expanded into diverse scenarios, enabling models to internalize safety-oriented values and principles rather than explicit refusal patterns. Experiments across multiple Base and Instruct models show that SSRFT achieves more robust and generalizable safety alignment than standard SFT. SSRFT shows substantially greater robustness to prefilling attacks and better generalization to unseen jailbreak domains, while reducing over-refusal on benign queries and preserving the model's general capabilities. These results establish safe-role internalization as an effective alternative to refusal-centric safety alignment. Warning: This paper contains examples of harmful and toxic language.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越拒绝模式：稳健且可推广的LLM安全对齐的安全角色内化
大型语言模型（LLMs）已实现显著能力，但仍易受到越狱攻击，导致有害或不安全的输出。现有的安全对齐方法，包括监督微调（SFT）和基于人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF），通常需要大量特定攻击的监督和计算资源，同时仍然容易受到浅层安全对齐和过度拒绝的影响。为了解决这些挑战，我们引入了SSRFT（监督安全角色微调），这是第一个将安全对齐重新定义为预定义安全角色内化的框架。SSRFT从心理测量问题、有限的越狱提示和安全角色描述中构建了安全角色问答（SRQA）数据集。合角色一致的响应被合成、验证并扩展到多样化场景，使模型能够内化以安全为导向的价值观和原则，而不是明确的拒绝模式。多个基础和指令模型的实验表明，SSRFT实现了比标准SFT更稳健和可推广的安全对齐。SSRFT对预填充攻击表现出显著更大的鲁棒性，并在未见过的越狱领域中更好地泛化，同时减少了对良性查询的过度拒绝，并保持了模型的整体能力。这些结果确立了安全角色内化作为拒绝中心安全对齐的有效替代方案。警告：本文包含有害和有毒语言的示例。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the vulnerabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) to jailbreak attacks that can produce harmful outputs, highlighting the limitations of existing safety alignment methods such as Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF), which often require extensive supervision and are prone to shallow alignment and over-refusal. The proposed method, Supervised Safe-Role Fine-Tuning (SSRFT), distinguishes itself by reformulating safety alignment as the internalization of a predefined safe role, utilizing a Safe-Role Question-Answer (SRQA) dataset that combines psychometric questions and safe-role descriptions to promote safety-oriented values instead of mere refusal patterns. This paper contributes a novel framework that demonstrates improved robustness and generalizability in safety alignment, achieving superior performance against prefilling attacks and better adaptability to new jailbreak scenarios while minimizing over-refusal on benign queries. The experiments conducted across various Base and Instruct models validate that SSRFT effectively enhances safety alignment without compromising the models' general capabilities.

All you need to break LLMs are Black-Box, Adapting, Efficient, Transferable, Harmful, Applicable ... Attacks
Authors: Vincent Limbach, Jonas Dornbusch, David Lüdke, Stephan Günnemann, Leo Schwinn
First: 2026-06-02T13:39:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-02T12:35:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurately evaluating adversarial robustness is a longstanding challenge. A flawed attack design can inflate robustness estimates, making deployment risk assessment and defense comparison unreliable. Historically, standardized attacks such as AutoAttack have largely resolved this for image classifiers, providing a reliable evaluation baseline for systematic comparison across defenses. However, no equivalent exists for LLM jailbreak evaluation yet, where designing such an attack is considerably more difficult. A reliable attack must, among other things, be black-box compatible, applicable to arbitrary defense pipelines, and efficient, which no existing method jointly satisfies. We introduce Indirect Harmfulness Optimization (IHO), a masked diffusion language model attacker trained via iterative preference optimization against a harmfulness judge, requiring only black-box access to the target. The same method can be used without modification as a strong adapting attack on individual behaviors, or as an efficient amortized policy that transfers to held-out behaviors and unseen target models without fine-tuning. Even against layered defenses, such as GLM 5.3-flash served through a black-box API deploying an auxiliary detector, IHO improves attack success considerably over state-of-the-art approaches, without any defense-specific adaptation. Our results position IHO as a practical step toward the kind of standardized jailbreak evaluation that has improved reliability in the past. Code and models are available on GitHub and Hugging Face.
中文标题/摘要
标题：破解LLM所需的仅仅是黑箱、适应性、有效、可转移、有害、适用……攻击
准确评估对抗鲁棒性一直是一个长期挑战。设计不当的攻击可能会夸大鲁棒性估计，使得部署风险评估和防御比较不可靠。历史上，标准化攻击如AutoAttack在图像分类器中基本解决了这个问题，为系统比较提供了可靠的评估基准。然而，目前尚无等效的LLM越狱评估方法，设计这样的攻击要困难得多。一个可靠的攻击必须在其他条件下兼容黑箱，适用于任意防御管道，并且高效，而现有方法没有一个能够共同满足这些要求。我们引入了间接有害性优化（IHO），这是一种通过对抗有害性评估者进行迭代偏好优化训练的掩蔽扩散语言模型攻击者，仅需对目标进行黑箱访问。相同的方法可以在不修改的情况下作为对个体行为的强适应性攻击，或作为一种高效的摊销策略，能够转移到保留行为和未见目标模型上，而无需微调。即使面对分层防御，例如通过黑箱API部署辅助检测器的GLM 5.3-flash，IHO在攻击成功率上显著超过了最先进的方法，而无需任何特定于防御的适应。我们的结果将IHO定位为朝着标准化越狱评估的实用步骤，这在过去提高了可靠性。代码和模型可在GitHub和Hugging Face上获取。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the challenge of accurately evaluating adversarial robustness in large language models (LLMs), highlighting that existing attack designs can lead to inflated robustness estimates, which complicate risk assessment and defense comparisons. Previous methods, such as standardized attacks for image classifiers, do not translate effectively to LLMs due to the complexities involved in designing effective attacks that are black-box compatible and applicable across various defense mechanisms. The proposed Indirect Harmfulness Optimization (IHO) method overcomes these limitations by utilizing a masked diffusion language model attacker that requires only black-box access to the target, allowing it to function as both a strong adapting attack and an efficient amortized policy for unseen models. The paper demonstrates that IHO significantly enhances attack success rates against advanced defenses, establishing it as a promising approach for standardized jailbreak evaluation in LLMs, thereby contributing to more reliable assessments of adversarial robustness.
本研究解决了准确评估大型语言模型（LLMs）对抗鲁棒性的问题，强调现有攻击设计可能导致鲁棒性估计膨胀，从而使风险评估和防御比较变得复杂。以往的方法，如针对图像分类器的标准化攻击，尚未成功适用于LLM越狱评估，因为设计有效攻击的复杂性使得黑箱兼容性和适用于各种防御机制的要求难以满足。提出的间接有害性优化（IHO）方法通过利用仅需黑箱访问目标的掩蔽扩散语言模型攻击者，克服了这些限制，使其既可以作为适应性攻击，也可以作为高效的策略，能够在不进行微调的情况下转移到新行为。论文表明，IHO显著提高了对分层防御的攻击成功率，确立了其作为标准化越狱评估的可行方法，从而为LLMs对抗鲁棒性的更可靠评估做出了贡献。
Scalable, Transferable Meta-network for Data Selection Requires a Different Loss (and Why the Obvious Choice is Problematic)
Authors: Zilin Du, Bowen Yang, Boyang Albert Li
First: 2026-10-01T17:23:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-01T17:23:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Data selection is critical for training large language models on massive and heterogeneous corpora. Meta-learning for Training-data Selection offers a principled alternative to heuristic scoring by learning data weights from a target validation objective, but existing methods face a trade-off between fine-grained valuation and transferability to unseen data. A natural solution is to replace per-sample weights with a selection network. However, we find that directly incorporating such a network into existing MTS objectives leads to unstable optimization and poor generalization, caused by weight suppression and persistent reliance on easy-to-learn features. To address these issues, we propose Transferable Example Scoring and Selection (TESS), a scalable data-selection framework built on a Pointwise Value Matching objective (PVM). Experiments on LLM safety and targeted instruction tuning demonstrate strong transfer across datasets, from subsets to full corpora, and from smaller to larger models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：可扩展的可转移元网络用于数据选择需要不同的损失（以及为什么明显的选择存在问题）
数据选择对于在大规模异构语料库上训练大型语言模型至关重要。用于训练数据选择的元学习提供了一种基于目标验证目标学习数据权重的原则性替代方案，但现有方法在细粒度评估和对未见数据的可转移性之间面临权衡。一个自然的解决方案是用选择网络替代每个样本的权重。然而，我们发现将这样的网络直接纳入现有的MTS目标会导致不稳定的优化和较差的泛化，原因在于权重抑制和对易学特征的持续依赖。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了可转移示例评分和选择（TESS），这是一个基于逐点值匹配目标（PVM）构建的可扩展数据选择框架。在LLM安全性和针对性指令调优的实验中，展示了从子集到完整语料库、从小模型到大模型的强转移。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the challenge of data selection for training large language models on diverse datasets, highlighting the limitations of existing meta-learning methods that struggle with balancing fine-grained data valuation and transferability to unseen data. Previous approaches often lead to unstable optimization and poor generalization due to issues like weight suppression and over-reliance on easily learned features. The proposed method, Transferable Example Scoring and Selection (TESS), introduces a scalable framework that utilizes a Pointwise Value Matching objective to improve data selection. This approach effectively enhances transferability and stability, as demonstrated by experiments on tasks related to LLM safety and targeted instruction tuning, achieving strong performance across various datasets and model sizes, thereby supporting the research goals.
本研究解决了在多样化数据集上训练大型语言模型时数据选择的挑战，强调了现有元学习方法在平衡细粒度数据评估和对新数据的可转移性方面的局限性。以往的方法常常导致优化不稳定和泛化能力差，原因在于权重抑制和过度依赖易学特征。所提出的方法——可转移示例评分与选择（TESS），引入了一种基于点对点价值匹配目标的可扩展框架，以克服这些挑战，为数据选择提供了更有效的解决方案。该方法在针对LLM安全性和目标指令调优的实验中表现出色，实现了在不同数据集和模型规模之间的有效转移，从而支持了改善数据选择的目标。
CHILLGuard: Towards Fine-Grained Chinese LLM Safety Guardrail with Scalable Data Construction and Model-aware Preference Alignment
Authors: Wenbo Yu, Bohua Wang, Hao Fang, Kuofeng Gao, Jingru Zeng, Xiaochen Yang, Tianyi Zhang, Xiaoxiao Ma, Jiawei Kong, Hao Wu, Bin Chen, Shu-Tao Xia, Min Zhang
Venue: EMNLP 2026
First: 2026-06-13T16:57:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-01T13:45:00+00:00
Comments: accepted by EMNLP 2026 findings
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Malicious content generated from large language models (LLMs) could pose severe safety risks and ethical concerns. While existing LLM safety guardrails excel in English or multilingual settings, they lack adaptation to Chinese-specific regulatory policies, cultural context, and linguistic nuances, failing to support fine-grained risk classification for diverse deployment needs. In this paper, we introduce a 5-macro, 31-micro category fine-grained risk taxonomy for Chinese scenarios, and build CHILLGuard: a dedicated Chinese LLM content safety guardrail. To address the critical scarcity of high-quality annotated Chinese safety data, we propose a scalable multi-stage data construction pipeline: we expand multi-source corpus via retrieval-augmented generation, generate implicit harmful samples through prompt engineering rewriting, and refine high-quality data via multi-model voting-based label calibration. Based on this, we build CHILLGuardTrain, a large-scale training set with 405,007 samples, and CHILLGuardTest, a rigorously curated annotated test set with 51,745 samples. We then train CHILLGuard on CHILLGuardTrain under a generator-classifier collaborative framework via Model-aware Direct Preference Optimization. Extensive experiments under multiple settings demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance of CHILLGuard, e.g., a 15.92% relative improvement of F1 score over Qwen3Guard-8B-Strict on our benchmark. We release our resources at https://github.com/cswbyu/CHILLGuard.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CHILLGuard：面向细粒度中文LLM安全防护的可扩展数据构建与模型感知偏好对齐
大型语言模型（LLMs）生成的恶意内容可能带来严重的安全风险和伦理问题。现有的LLM安全防护在英语或多语言环境中表现出色，但缺乏对中国特定监管政策、文化背景和语言细微差别的适应，无法支持多样化部署需求的细粒度风险分类。本文介绍了一种针对中文场景的5个宏观、31个微观类别的细粒度风险分类法，并构建了CHILLGuard：一个专门的中文LLM内容安全防护。为了解决高质量标注中文安全数据的严重短缺，我们提出了一种可扩展的多阶段数据构建流程：通过检索增强生成扩展多源语料，通过提示工程重写生成隐性有害样本，并通过多模型投票基础的标签校准精炼高质量数据。在此基础上，我们构建了CHILLGuardTrain，一个包含405,007个样本的大规模训练集，以及CHILLGuardTest，一个经过严格策划的包含51,745个样本的标注测试集。然后，我们在CHILLGuardTrain上通过模型感知直接偏好优化的生成器-分类器协作框架训练CHILLGuard。多种设置下的广泛实验表明，CHILLGuard的性能达到了最先进水平，例如，在我们的基准测试中，相较于Qwen3Guard-8B-Strict，F1分数提高了15.92%。我们在https://github.com/cswbyu/CHILLGuard发布我们的资源。
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the safety risks and ethical concerns posed by malicious content generated from large language models (LLMs), particularly in the context of Chinese language and culture, where existing safety guardrails are inadequate. Previous methods have focused primarily on English or multilingual settings, lacking the necessary adaptation to Chinese-specific regulatory policies and linguistic nuances, which limits their effectiveness in fine-grained risk classification. The proposed approach, CHILLGuard, introduces a comprehensive fine-grained risk taxonomy tailored for Chinese scenarios and employs a scalable multi-stage data construction pipeline to generate high-quality annotated safety data. This methodology includes retrieval-augmented generation, prompt engineering for harmful sample generation, and multi-model voting for label calibration, resulting in a large-scale training set of 405,007 samples and a test set of 51,745 samples. The experimental results demonstrate that CHILLGuard achieves state-of-the-art performance, with a 15.92% relative improvement in F1 score compared to existing models, thereby supporting its goal of enhancing content safety in Chinese LLM applications.
本研究关注大型语言模型（LLMs）生成的恶意内容所带来的安全风险和伦理问题，特别是在中文语言和文化背景下，现有的安全防护措施不足。以往的方法主要集中在英语或多语言环境中，缺乏对中文特定监管政策和语言细微差别的适应，限制了其在细粒度风险分类中的有效性。本文提出了CHILLGuard，一个专门针对中文的LLM内容安全防护工具，并通过可扩展的多阶段数据构建流程支持其开发，该流程包括检索增强生成和提示工程以生成有害样本，最终形成了一个包含405,007个样本的大规模训练集。该方法论通过生成器-分类器协作框架与模型感知直接偏好优化训练CHILLGuard，在基准测试中实现了相较于现有模型F1分数提高15.92%的先进性能，从而满足中文LLM应用中对强大安全机制的需求。
In Vino Veritas and Vulnerabilities: Examining LLM Safety via Drunk Language Inducement
Authors: Anudeex Shetty, Aditya Joshi, Salil S. Kanhere
First: 2026-01-19T12:44:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-01T12:14:18+00:00
Comments: Accepted to INLG 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Humans are susceptible to undesirable behaviours and privacy leaks under the influence of alcohol. This paper investigates drunk language, i.e., text written under the influence of alcohol, as a driver for safety failures in large language models (LLMs). We investigate three mechanisms for inducing drunk language in LLMs: persona-based prompting, causal fine-tuning, and reinforcement-based post-training. When evaluated on 5 LLMs, we observe a higher susceptibility to jailbreaking on JailbreakBench (even in the presence of defences) and privacy leaks on ConfAIde, where both benchmarks are in English, as compared to the base LLMs as well as previously reported approaches. Via a robust combination of manual evaluation and LLM-based evaluators and analysis of error categories, our findings highlight a correspondence between human-intoxicated behaviour, and anthropomorphism in LLMs induced with drunk language. The simplicity and efficiency of our drunk language inducement approaches position them as potential counters for LLM safety tuning, highlighting significant risks to LLM safety.
中文标题/摘要
标题：酒中真理与脆弱性：通过醉酒语言诱导考察大型语言模型的安全性
人类在酒精影响下容易出现不良行为和隐私泄露。本文研究醉酒语言，即在酒精影响下撰写的文本，作为大型语言模型（LLMs）安全失败的驱动因素。我们探讨了在LLMs中诱导醉酒语言的三种机制：基于角色的提示、因果微调和基于强化的后训练。在对5个LLMs进行评估时，我们观察到在JailbreakBench上（即使在防御存在的情况下）对越狱的更高敏感性，以及在ConfAIde上隐私泄露的情况，这两个基准均为英语，相较于基础LLMs和之前报告的方法。通过手动评估与基于LLM的评估者的稳健组合，以及对错误类别的分析，我们的发现突显了人类醉酒行为与通过醉酒语言诱导的LLMs中的拟人化之间的对应关系。我们诱导醉酒语言的方法简单高效，使其成为LLM安全调优的潜在对策，突显了对LLM安全的重大风险。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the safety vulnerabilities of large language models (LLMs) by exploring the concept of drunk language, which refers to text generated under the influence of alcohol. Previous methods have not adequately examined the impact of induced language variations on LLM safety, leading to gaps in understanding their susceptibility to harmful behaviors and privacy breaches. The proposed approach introduces three mechanisms for inducing drunk language—persona-based prompting, causal fine-tuning, and reinforcement-based post-training—effectively demonstrating their potential to expose safety failures in LLMs. The research methodology involves evaluating five LLMs against benchmarks like JailbreakBench and ConfAIde, revealing increased susceptibility to jailbreaking and privacy leaks compared to baseline models. The findings underscore the significant risks posed by LLMs when influenced by drunk language, suggesting that these methods could serve as effective tools for enhancing LLM safety tuning.

False Floors: LLM Safety Routing Evaluations Break Under Distribution Shift
Authors: Amit Singh Bhatti, Vishal Vaddina
First: 2026-10-01T12:09:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-01T12:09:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Safety routers send each request to one of several models and are judged against the best single model. A major routing benchmark picks that comparator on the evaluation data. In the benchmark's own setting this is harmless, but under distribution shift it is not. On HELM Safety the selection cost is 0.003-0.030 of harm under random splits and 0.045-0.113 under held-out categories, comparable to the whole deficit attributed to routing, with its direction holding under either published judge alone. It rises seven- to ninefold on AgentDojo when suites are held out. Across seven safety corpora chosen by rules fixed in advance, three meet a registered interval test and four beat a later permutation null, and three of the four interval misses are corpora where some models have zero observed harm. Prior work proves the direction of this bias. We size it on harm and accuracy, show that it is larger under the held-out splits we measure, and bound it by optimism plus a shift-dependent regret. Scored honestly under shift, routing buys little on these benchmarks. In most pool cells the nested router serves the honest baseline's model, and on the nearly saturated AgentDojo corpus a perfect pre-dispatch router is worth at most two points of harm. We also find a model's expressed recognition of a late injection steerable. On held-out reruns an attacker who knows which model it faces lowers GPT-5.4's judged recognition by 19.6 points, confirmed by an independent label. In an offline counterfactual composition into a controller, the same attack raises or lowers estimated harm depending on the fallback model. Safety routing should be evaluated under shift, against a baseline chosen without the test labels, and recognition-based defences should be scored on harm against an attacker who chooses what the model sees.
中文标题/摘要
标题：虚假底层：LLM安全路由评估在分布变化下失效
安全路由器将每个请求发送到多个模型中的一个，并与最佳单一模型进行比较。一个主要的路由基准在评估数据中选择该比较器。在基准自身的设置中这是无害的，但在分布变化下则不是。在HELM安全中，选择成本在随机拆分下为0.003-0.030的损害，在保留类别下为0.045-0.113，与路由所归因的整体缺口相当，且在单独发布的评审下方向保持不变。当套件被保留时，在AgentDojo上成本增加七到九倍。在七个由预先固定规则选择的安全语料库中，三个满足注册区间测试，四个超过后来的置换零假设，且四个区间缺失中的三个是某些模型观察到的损害为零的语料库。先前的工作证明了这种偏差的方向。我们在损害和准确性上对其进行了量化，显示在我们测量的保留拆分下更大，并通过乐观加上依赖于变化的遗憾进行界定。在变化下诚实评分的路由在这些基准上几乎没有收益。在大多数池单元中，嵌套路由器服务于诚实基线的模型，而在几乎饱和的AgentDojo语料库中，一个完美的预调度路由器最多值两个损害点。我们还发现一个模型对晚期注入的表达识别是可操控的。在保留重跑中，知道面对哪个模型的攻击者将GPT-5.4的评估识别降低了19.6点，得到了独立标签的确认。在离线反事实组合到控制器中时，相同的攻击根据后备模型的不同提高或降低估计的损害。安全路由应在变化下进行评估，与未使用测试标签选择的基线进行比较，基于识别的防御应在面对选择模型所见内容的攻击者时按损害进行评分。
Summary / 总结
This article addresses the limitations of existing safety routers that evaluate requests by comparing them to a single best model, particularly under conditions of distribution shift. Previous methods have been shown to be inadequate as they do not account for the biases introduced by the evaluation data, leading to significant harm in routing decisions. The proposed approach emphasizes the need for safety routing evaluations to be conducted under distribution shifts, using a more robust methodology that includes honest scoring against a baseline not influenced by test labels. The research methodology involves analyzing performance across seven safety corpora and demonstrating that the biases in routing can lead to substantial increases in harm, particularly in held-out scenarios. The findings indicate that traditional routing methods provide minimal benefits under these conditions, with the proposed evaluation framework revealing the true extent of harm and accuracy, thus supporting the need for improved safety routing mechanisms.
本研究解决了现有安全路由器的局限性，这些路由器通过将请求与单一最佳模型进行比较来评估请求，特别是在分布变化的情况下，这种方法变得问题重重。以往的方法未能充分考虑这种变化引入的偏差，导致模型性能的评估存在误导。所提出的方法强调在分布变化下进行安全路由评估的必要性，使用不依赖于测试标签的更稳健基线。本文的贡献在于量化路由评估中的偏差，并证明传统基准在模型在变化分布下评估时可能显著低估危害。该方法论涉及分析安全语料库，并在各种场景中测量危害和准确性，结果表明，在这些背景下，路由提供的好处微乎其微，发现完美的路由模型在饱和数据集中仅能略微减少危害。这些结果支持在安全路由中修订评估标准的必要性，以确保在现实条件下对模型性能的准确评估。
TensorCommitments: A Lightweight Verifiable Inference for Language Models
Authors: Oguzhan Baser, Elahe Sadeghi, Eric Wang, Nico Vergauwen, Sam Kazemian, Hong Kang, Sandeep P. Chinchali, Sriram Vishwanath
First: 2026-02-13T05:23:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-01T09:29:49+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 8 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Most large language models (LLMs) run on external clouds: users send a prompt, pay for inference, and must trust that the remote GPU executes the LLM without any adversarial tampering. We critically ask how to achieve verifiable LLM inference, where a prover (the service) must convince a verifier (the client) that an inference was run correctly without rerunning the LLM. Existing cryptographic works are too slow at the LLM scale, while non-cryptographic ones require a strong verifier GPU. We propose TensorCommitments (TCs), a tensor-native proof-of-inference scheme. TC binds the LLM inference to a commitment, an irreversible tag that breaks under tampering, organized in our multivariate Terkle Trees. For LLaMA2, TC adds only 0.97% prover and 0.12% verifier time over inference while improving robustness to tailored LLM attacks by up to 48% over the best prior work requiring a verifier GPU.
中文标题/摘要
标题：张量承诺：一种轻量级可验证的语言模型推理
大多数大型语言模型（LLMs）运行在外部云上：用户发送提示，支付推理费用，并必须信任远程GPU在没有任何对抗性篡改的情况下执行LLM。我们批判性地询问如何实现可验证的LLM推理，其中证明者（服务）必须说服验证者（客户端）在不重新运行LLM的情况下推理是正确的。现有的密码学工作在LLM规模上速度太慢，而非密码学的方案则需要强大的验证者GPU。我们提出了张量承诺（TC），一种张量原生的推理证明方案。TC将LLM推理绑定到承诺上，这是一个在篡改下会破坏的不可逆标签，组织在我们的多变量特克尔树中。对于LLaMA2，TC在推理上仅增加0.97%的证明者时间和0.12%的验证者时间，同时将针对定制LLM攻击的鲁棒性提高了多达48%，相比于需要验证者GPU的最佳先前工作。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the challenge of ensuring verifiable inference for large language models (LLMs) that operate on external clouds, where users must trust the service to execute the model correctly without tampering. Previous methods, including cryptographic approaches, are often too slow for LLMs, while non-cryptographic solutions necessitate powerful verifier GPUs, limiting their applicability. The proposed TensorCommitments (TCs) scheme offers a tensor-native proof-of-inference that binds LLM inference to a commitment, utilizing multivariate Terkle Trees to enhance security without the need for extensive computational resources. This method significantly improves robustness against tailored LLM attacks by up to 48% compared to existing solutions, while adding minimal overhead of 0.97% for the prover and 0.12% for the verifier during inference, thus supporting the goal of efficient and secure LLM deployment.
本研究解决了在外部云上运行的大型语言模型（LLMs）中确保可验证推理的挑战，用户必须信任服务正确执行模型而不被篡改。以往的方法，包括加密方法，对于LLMs来说速度过慢，而非加密方法则需要强大的验证者GPU，限制了其实用性。提出的张量承诺（TCs）方案提供了一种张量原生的推理证明，将LLM推理绑定到承诺上，使用多变量特克尔树增强对篡改的安全性。该方法的动机充分，因为它显著减少了证明者和验证者的时间开销，同时在与现有方法相比提高了对攻击的鲁棒性，提升幅度可达48%。该方法论表明，TCs可以与LLaMA2集成，性能影响极小，实现了验证效率与安全性的平衡。
Easier Said Than Done: Unpacking Intent-Behavior Gap in Jailbreaking LLM-Based Robots
Authors: Xuancun Lu, Zhengxian Huang, Xinfeng Li, Chi Zhang, Xiaoyu Ji, Wenyuan Xu
First: 2024-12-21T13:58:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-01T01:33:58+00:00
Comments: Accepted by NDSS 2027
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
LLM-based robots use Large Language Models (LLMs) as planners to translate natural language instructions into policies such as grasp(), move_to(), and open_gripper(). Jailbreak attacks on these robots extend the threat from generating malicious content to executing harmful behaviors. However, we find that existing jailbreak attempts against LLM-based robots that produce malicious-looking policies (intent jailbreaks) often fail to induce harmful physical actions by robots (behavior jailbreaks), due to robot-specific constraints, such as logical errors and hallucinated control APIs. In this paper, we demystify the intent-behavior gap and investigate its root causes to inform effective defenses. Our measurement study finds that current LLM jailbreak methods overlook robot-specific syntax constraints (e.g., executable control APIs) and physical feasibility (e.g., ordering of policies and hardware/kinematic constraints). To bridge the gap, we introduce POEF (POlicy EFfective Jailbreak), an automated red-teaming framework that takes into account the robot-specific constraints during both the optimization and evaluation processes. Specifically, POEF employs the hidden-layer gradients from an unaligned LLM to guide the jailbreak prompt optimization and uses a multi-agent evaluator to assess the feasibility of the generated policies. Experiments on commercial robots, including the Unitree G1, the Franka robotic arm, and simulators, show that POEF achieves an 80% behavior jailbreak success rate and transfers across various LLMs. In addition, we propose two defense strategies that mitigate the behavior jailbreak risks. Our findings indicate an urgent need for stronger countermeasures before LLM-based robots are deployed at scale. The homepage is available at https://zjushine.github.io/poef.github.io/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：说起来容易做起来难：揭示越狱 LLM 基于机器人的意图-行为差距
基于 LLM 的机器人使用大型语言模型 (LLM) 作为规划者，将自然语言指令转换为策略，如 grasp()、move_to() 和 open_gripper()。对这些机器人的越狱攻击将威胁从生成恶意内容扩展到执行有害行为。然而，我们发现，现有针对 LLM 基于机器人的越狱尝试（意图越狱）往往未能引发机器人（行为越狱）的有害物理动作，原因在于机器人特有的约束，如逻辑错误和虚构的控制 API。本文揭示了意图-行为差距，并调查其根本原因，以便为有效防御提供信息。我们的测量研究发现，当前的 LLM 越狱方法忽视了机器人特有的语法约束（例如，可执行控制 API）和物理可行性（例如，策略的顺序和硬件/运动学约束）。为了解决这一差距，我们引入了 POEF（策略有效越狱），这是一个自动化红队框架，在优化和评估过程中考虑机器人特有的约束。具体而言，POEF 利用未对齐 LLM 的隐藏层梯度来指导越狱提示优化，并使用多智能体评估器来评估生成策略的可行性。在包括 Unitree G1、Franka 机械臂和模拟器在内的商业机器人上的实验表明，POEF 实现了 80% 的行为越狱成功率，并在各种 LLM 之间转移。此外，我们提出了两种防御策略，以减轻行为越狱风险。我们的研究结果表明，在 LLM 基于机器人大规模部署之前，迫切需要更强的对策。主页可访问 https://zjushine.github.io/poef.github.io/。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the vulnerabilities of LLM-based robots to jailbreak attacks, which can lead to harmful behaviors despite existing attempts focusing on generating malicious policies. Previous methods often fail due to overlooking robot-specific constraints, such as syntax and physical feasibility, resulting in a disconnect between intended and actual behaviors. This paper introduces POEF (Policy Effective Jailbreak), an automated framework that incorporates these constraints during optimization and evaluation, significantly improving the effectiveness of jailbreak attempts. The methodology involves using hidden-layer gradients from an unaligned LLM for prompt optimization and a multi-agent evaluator for policy feasibility assessment. Experiments demonstrate that POEF achieves an 80% success rate in behavior jailbreaks across various commercial robots and simulators, highlighting the need for enhanced defenses before widespread deployment of LLM-based robots.
本研究关注LLM（大型语言模型）驱动的机器人面临的越狱攻击问题，这些攻击可能导致有害行为，尽管现有方法主要集中在生成恶意外观的策略上。以往的方法常常忽视机器人特定的约束条件，如控制API和物理可行性，从而导致意图与实际行为之间的脱节。提出的方法POEF（策略有效越狱）旨在通过在优化和评估过程中纳入这些约束来弥合这一意图-行为差距。POEF利用未对齐的LLM的隐藏层梯度进行提示优化，并采用多智能体评估器来评估策略的可行性。实验结果表明，POEF在各种商业机器人上实现了80%的行为越狱成功率，强调在LLM驱动的机器人广泛部署之前需要加强防御措施。
$D^2$-Monitor: Dynamic Safety Monitoring for Diffusion LLMs via Hesitation Signals
Authors: Aoxi Liu, Yupeng Chen, James Oldfield, Guanzhe Hong, Junchi Yu, Baoyuan Wu, Philip Torr, Adel Bibi
First: 2026-05-25T14:22:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-30T09:49:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite the emergence of diffusion large language models (D-LLMs) as an alternative to autoregressive large language models (AR-LLMs), safety monitoring for D-LLMs remains largely unexplored. Unlike AR-LLMs, D-LLMs generate text through a multi-step denoising process, exposing intermediate hidden representations that may contain safety-relevant information unavailable in standard single-step monitoring setups. Motivated by the suitability of lightweight probes for always-on monitoring, we analyze which trajectory-level signals best indicate when such probes are likely to struggle. We find that the most informative signal is safety hesitation: intermediate hidden states repeatedly falling within a small margin of the probe's decision boundary. The number of such hesitation steps in D-LLM's trajectory predicts probe failure effectively, providing a proxy for sample difficulty. Building on this analysis, we propose $D^2$-Monitor, in which a lightweight probe runs on every sample, producing a base prediction and a hesitation estimate that decides whether to escalate the sample to a more expressive probe. That probe is trained on trajectories with at least one hesitation step, and reads only the minimal span of those steps. Evaluated on 3 datasets (WildGuardMix, ToxicChat, OpenAI-Moderation) across 4 D-LLMs, $D^2$-Monitor achieves state-of-the-art performance with a compact parameter footprint ($\leq$ 0.93M parameters), and exhibits the best trade-off between effectiveness and efficiency relative to 8 baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：$D^2$-监测器：通过犹豫信号对扩散大语言模型进行动态安全监测
尽管扩散大语言模型（D-LLMs）作为自回归大语言模型（AR-LLMs）的替代方案已出现，但对D-LLMs的安全监测仍然 largely 未被探索。与AR-LLMs不同，D-LLMs通过多步骤去噪过程生成文本，暴露出可能包含安全相关信息的中间隐藏表示，这些信息在标准单步监测设置中不可用。基于轻量级探针适合于始终在线监测的动机，我们分析了哪些轨迹级信号最能指示这些探针可能遇到困难。我们发现，最具信息量的信号是安全犹豫：中间隐藏状态反复落在探针决策边界的小范围内。D-LLM轨迹中此类犹豫步骤的数量有效预测探针失败，提供了样本难度的代理。基于此分析，我们提出了$D^2$-监测器，其中轻量级探针在每个样本上运行，产生基本预测和犹豫估计，以决定是否将样本升级到更具表现力的探针。该探针在至少有一个犹豫步骤的轨迹上进行训练，并仅读取这些步骤的最小跨度。在3个数据集（WildGuardMix、ToxicChat、OpenAI-Moderation）上评估4个D-LLMs，$D^2$-监测器以紧凑的参数占用（≤ 0.93M参数）实现了最先进的性能，并在相对于8个基线之间展现了最佳的有效性与效率的权衡。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the gap in safety monitoring for diffusion large language models (D-LLMs), which have emerged as alternatives to autoregressive large language models (AR-LLMs). Previous methods primarily focused on AR-LLMs and did not leverage the unique multi-step denoising process of D-LLMs, leading to inadequate safety monitoring. The proposed approach, $D^2$-Monitor, utilizes lightweight probes to continuously monitor D-LLMs by analyzing trajectory-level signals, particularly safety hesitation, which indicates potential probe failure. This method effectively predicts sample difficulty and enhances monitoring accuracy. The contribution of the paper lies in the development of $D^2$-Monitor, which operates with a compact parameter footprint and achieves state-of-the-art performance across three datasets (WildGuardMix, ToxicChat, OpenAI-Moderation) and four D-LLMs, demonstrating a superior balance between effectiveness and efficiency compared to eight baseline methods.
本研究解决了扩散大型语言模型（D-LLMs）安全监控的空白，这些模型通过多步骤去噪过程生成文本，与自回归大型语言模型（AR-LLMs）不同，能够揭示中间隐藏表示。以往的方法未能有效利用这些中间状态，导致安全监控不足。所提出的方法 $D^2$-Monitor 利用轻量级探针持续监控 D-LLMs，通过分析轨迹级信号，特别关注安全犹豫，这表明潜在的探针失败。该方法通过基于犹豫步骤预测样本难度，必要时允许升级到更具表现力的探针，从而增强监控能力。本文的贡献在于在三个数据集（WildGuardMix、ToxicChat、OpenAI-Moderation）上实现了最先进的性能，参数量不足 0.93 百万，展示了与八个基线方法相比在效率和有效性之间的良好平衡。
Faithful Dual-constrained Erasure for Robust LLM Safety Alignment
Authors: Jiaqing Li, Shide Zhou, Zhibo Zhang, Yuxi Li, Tianlong Yu, Kailong Wang
First: 2026-09-30T08:29:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-30T08:29:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Machine unlearning has emerged as a crucial mechanism for removing hazardous knowledge and enforcing safety alignment in Large Language Models (LLMs). However, recent studies reveal a persistent security risk: unlearned models remain highly vulnerable to retraining attacks, where suppressed malicious behaviors rapidly resurface after benign fine-tuning. In this work, we investigate the optimization dynamics of unlearning and identify that this vulnerability stems from shallow alignment. Rather than effectively erasing target knowledge, models often exploit a shortcut by activating previously dormant parameters to act as spurious suppressors, forming a fragile inhibitory shell over intact malicious representations. To address this issue and enforce authentic memory deletion, we propose FDCU, a novel dual-constrained subspace projection framework. FDCU restricts parameter updates through a highly scalable, element-wise dual-masking rule: it preserves general knowledge manifolds via Fisher Information and strictly prohibits the abnormal activation of spurious suppressors via the Principle of Minimal Functional Intervention (PMFI). By reliably blocking the model's ability to superficially hide knowledge, FDCU promotes the authentic dismantling of target representations. Extensive experiments across specific knowledge erasure and safe output control tasks demonstrate that FDCU achieves state-of-the-art robustness against retraining attacks while maintaining near-lossless general utility, ensuring durable safety for LLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：可靠的双约束消除机制用于强健的LLM安全对齐
机器遗忘已成为去除危险知识和强化大型语言模型（LLM）安全对齐的重要机制。然而，最近的研究揭示了一个持续的安全风险：被遗忘的模型仍然高度脆弱于再训练攻击，在良性微调后被抑制的恶意行为迅速复发。在本研究中，我们探讨了遗忘的优化动态，并确定这种脆弱性源于浅层对齐。模型往往通过激活先前休眠的参数作为虚假抑制器来利用捷径，而不是有效地消除目标知识，从而在完整的恶意表征上形成脆弱的抑制壳。为了解决这个问题并强制执行真实的记忆删除，我们提出了FDCU，一种新颖的双约束子空间投影框架。FDCU通过高度可扩展的逐元素双掩蔽规则限制参数更新：它通过费舍尔信息保留一般知识流形，并严格禁止通过最小功能干预原则（PMFI）异常激活虚假抑制器。通过可靠地阻止模型表面隐藏知识的能力，FDCU促进了目标表征的真实拆解。在特定知识消除和安全输出控制任务中的广泛实验表明，FDCU在抵御再训练攻击方面实现了最先进的鲁棒性，同时保持近乎无损的一般效用，确保了LLM的持久安全。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the critical issue of safety alignment in Large Language Models (LLMs) through machine unlearning, which has been shown to leave models vulnerable to retraining attacks that can reactivate suppressed harmful behaviors. Previous methods inadequately erase target knowledge, often allowing models to exploit dormant parameters, resulting in a fragile suppression of malicious representations. The proposed approach, FDCU, introduces a dual-constrained subspace projection framework that effectively enforces genuine memory deletion by employing a scalable dual-masking rule that preserves essential knowledge while preventing the activation of spurious suppressors. This method is well-motivated as it directly tackles the identified vulnerabilities in existing unlearning techniques. The paper contributes by demonstrating that FDCU achieves state-of-the-art robustness against retraining attacks while maintaining near-lossless general utility in specific knowledge erasure and safe output control tasks, thereby supporting the goal of durable safety for LLMs.
本研究解决了大型语言模型（LLMs）中机器遗忘的关键问题，特别是未学习模型在重新训练攻击下的脆弱性，这使得被抑制的恶意行为重新出现。以往的方法未能有效消除有害知识，往往导致恶意表征的表面抑制而非真正删除。所提出的方法FDCU引入了一种双约束子空间投影框架，利用可扩展的双掩蔽规则确保真实的记忆删除，同时保留一般知识。该方法提高了LLMs对重新训练攻击的鲁棒性，并保持其一般效用。实验结果表明，FDCU在知识遗忘和安全输出控制任务中实现了最先进的性能，成功支持了LLMs持久安全对齐的目标。
How to Tame a Multi-Headed Hydra? Adaptive Multi-Category Safety Steering for Large Language Models
Authors: Chenxi Wang, Ruiyang Huang, Li Huang, Yifan Wu
First: 2026-09-28T07:53:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-29T07:14:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As large language models (LLMs) become increasingly widespread, preventing unsafe responses to harmful prompts is essential for their safe deployment. Activation steering offers an approach to improving LLM safety by modifying internal activations during inference without updating model parameters. However, a single prompt can involve multiple harm categories, and steering toward safety in one category may leave harmful content from another unaddressed. Despite advances in adaptive steering, existing methods do not explicitly coordinate steering direction and strength when multiple harm categories co-occur within a single prompt. To address this problem, we propose CAM-Steer, a Category-Adaptive Multi-category Safety Steering framework. Specifically, it estimates the risk associated with each harm category by comparing the current hidden state with safe and unsafe prototypes. The estimated risks are then used to combine the safety directions for different harm categories into a single steering direction and to determine the strength of the intervention. Finally, it rotates the hidden state along the composed steering direction, with the rotation angle determined by the estimated risks, while preserving the hidden-state norm. Experiments across three LLM backbones and seven harm categories show that CAM-Steer outperforms the evaluated baselines in average defense success rate, including when categories co-occur. Further analyses support its component designs and informative risk scores, with negligible inference overhead.
中文标题/摘要
标题：如何驯服多头水蛇？大型语言模型的自适应多类别安全引导
随着大型语言模型（LLMs）的广泛应用，防止对有害提示的安全响应对于其安全部署至关重要。激活引导提供了一种通过在推理过程中修改内部激活而不更新模型参数来提高LLM安全性的方法。然而，单个提示可能涉及多个危害类别，而在一个类别中引导安全可能会忽视另一个类别的有害内容。尽管自适应引导有所进展，但现有方法在多个危害类别同时出现时并未明确协调引导方向和强度。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了CAM-Steer，一个类别自适应多类别安全引导框架。具体而言，它通过将当前隐藏状态与安全和不安全原型进行比较来估计与每个危害类别相关的风险。然后，利用估计的风险将不同危害类别的安全方向组合成一个单一的引导方向，并确定干预的强度。最后，它沿着组合的引导方向旋转隐藏状态，旋转角度由估计的风险决定，同时保持隐藏状态的范数。针对三个LLM骨干和七个危害类别的实验表明，CAM-Steer在平均防御成功率上优于评估的基线，包括类别共现时。进一步分析支持其组件设计和信息丰富的风险评分，推理开销微乎其微。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the critical need for safety in large language models (LLMs) as their deployment increases, particularly in preventing unsafe responses to harmful prompts. Previous methods, such as activation steering, modify internal activations during inference but fail to effectively manage multiple harm categories that may arise from a single prompt, leading to incomplete safety measures. The proposed CAM-Steer framework improves upon these methods by estimating risks associated with each harm category and combining safety directions into a unified steering direction, thus ensuring a more comprehensive safety intervention. This methodology involves comparing hidden states with safe and unsafe prototypes to determine intervention strength and direction while maintaining hidden-state norms. Experiments demonstrate that CAM-Steer significantly enhances the average defense success rate across various LLM backbones and harm categories, effectively addressing the identified shortcomings of existing approaches.
本研究关注大型语言模型（LLMs）安全部署的关键需求，旨在防止对有害提示的安全响应。以往的方法，尤其是激活引导，虽然提高了LLM的安全性，但在处理涉及多个危害类别的提示时存在困难，因为它们未能有效协调引导方向和强度。提出的CAM-Steer框架通过评估每个危害类别的风险并将其整合为统一的引导方向，从而解决了现有方法的局限性。本文的贡献在于提出了一种基于风险评估的安全方向组合的新方法，并通过在三个LLM基础模型和七个危害类别上的实验，展示了其有效性，达到了比基线更高的平均防御成功率，即使在多个类别同时存在的情况下，同时保持了较低的推理开销。
When Do Model Internals Help? Exploring the Role of Representation Engineering in LLM Safety
Authors: Tianyi Guan, Jianhui Chen, Liangming Pan
First: 2026-09-28T09:50:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-28T09:50:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reliable AI safeguards require both control mechanisms that reduce unsafe behavior and monitoring mechanisms that detect safety risks during model interactions. Established behavioral safeguards include alignment methods that optimize model outputs and text monitors that assess interaction text. Representation engineering instead reads or modifies internal model states, but the relative strengths of these approaches remain unclear because they are often evaluated under different settings. We present a matched evaluation across two tracks. For safety control, we compare DPO, a behavioral alignment method, with three representation steering methods across robustness, practicality, and granularity. DPO provides the strongest overall control and generally improves with increasing training data, although its safety can degrade after subsequent benign fine-tuning. Representation steering remains competitive primarily in low-data settings, particularly with high-quality contrastive data. For safety monitoring, we compare representation probes with fine-tuned and open-weight text monitors across full-response detection, early detection, and computational cost. Specialized text monitors achieve the strongest overall detection accuracy, while representation probes remain competitive at substantially lower marginal cost. Finally, monitor-guided interventions recover much of the safety lost by DPO after benign fine-tuning, with little additional over-refusal. Overall, representation engineering does not generally replace behavioral safeguards, but offers practical advantages under specific conditions and can provide complementary safety benefits.
中文标题/摘要
标题：模型内部何时有帮助？探索表征工程在大型语言模型安全性中的作用
可靠的人工智能安全措施需要控制机制以减少不安全行为，以及监测机制以在模型交互中检测安全风险。既定的行为安全措施包括优化模型输出的对齐方法和评估交互文本的文本监测器。表征工程则读取或修改内部模型状态，但这些方法的相对优势仍不清楚，因为它们通常在不同的设置下进行评估。我们在两个轨道上进行了匹配评估。对于安全控制，我们比较了行为对齐方法DPO与三种表征引导方法在鲁棒性、实用性和粒度方面的表现。DPO提供了最强的整体控制，并且通常随着训练数据的增加而改善，尽管在后续的良性微调后，其安全性可能会下降。表征引导在低数据设置中仍然具有竞争力，特别是在高质量对比数据的情况下。对于安全监测，我们比较了表征探针与微调和开放权重的文本监测器在完整响应检测、早期检测和计算成本方面的表现。专业的文本监测器实现了最强的整体检测准确性，而表征探针在边际成本显著较低的情况下仍然具有竞争力。最后，监测引导的干预措施恢复了DPO在良性微调后失去的大部分安全性，几乎没有额外的拒绝。总体而言，表征工程通常并不取代行为安全措施，但在特定条件下提供了实用优势，并可以提供互补的安全益处。

ArGuard Shared Task: Harmful Content Detection in Arabic Memes and LLM Prompts
Authors: Firoj Alam, Md. Rafiul Biswas, Mohamed Bayan Kmainasi, Ali Ezzat Shahroor, Hamdy Mubarak, George Mikros, Abul Hasnat, Wajdi Zaghouani
First: 2026-09-24T10:22:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-25T14:21:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
ArGuard is a shared task on harmful content detection in Arabic memes and LLM prompts. It includes two tracks: Track A focuses on multimodal hate detection in Arabic memes, while Track B addresses harmful prompt detection for Arabic LLM safety evaluation. In total, 58 teams registered, 35 participated in the final evaluation, and 27 submitted system-description papers. Participating teams explored models such as AraBERT, Jais, and Qwen3-VL. The best systems achieved macro-F1 scores of 0.823 on A1, 0.419 on A2, 0.984 on B1, and 0.790 on B2. Fine-grained meme classification in A2 was the most challenging setting, partly due to sparse labels and train-test distribution shifts.

How Many Iterations to Jailbreak? Dynamic Budget Allocation for Multi-Turn LLM Evaluation
Authors: Shai Feldman, Yaniv Romano
First: 2026-05-07T17:25:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-24T19:53:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Evaluating and predicting the performance of large language models (LLMs) in multi-turn conversational settings is critical yet computationally expensive; key events---e.g., jailbreaks or successful task completion by an agent---often emerge only after repeated interactions. These events might be rare and, under any feasible computational budget, remain unobserved. Recent conformal survival frameworks construct reliable lower predictive bounds (LPBs) on the number of iterations to trigger the event of interest, but rely on static budget allocation that prohibits adaptivity in multi-turn setups. To address this, we introduce Dynamic Allocation via PRojected Optimization (DAPRO), a theoretically valid dynamic budget allocation framework for bounding the time-to-event in multi-turn LLM interactions. We prove that DAPRO satisfies the expected budget constraint and provides distribution-free, finite-sample coverage guarantees without requiring the conditional independence between censoring and event times assumed by prior conformal survival approaches. A key theoretical contribution is a novel coverage bound that scales with the square root of the mean censoring weight over only a subset of the samples rather than the worst-case weight, yielding tighter guarantees than prior work. Furthermore, DAPRO can be employed to obtain unbiased estimates of population-level evaluation metrics, such as the jailbreak rate, under limited computing resources. Comprehensive experiments across agentic task success, LLM jailbreaks, toxic content generation, and RAG hallucinations demonstrate that DAPRO achieves coverage closer to the nominal level with lower variance than static baselines, while not exceeding the budget constraint in expectation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：越狱需要多少次迭代？多轮LLM评估的动态预算分配
在多轮对话环境中评估和预测大型语言模型（LLM）的性能至关重要，但计算成本高；关键事件——例如越狱或代理成功完成任务——通常仅在反复交互后出现。这些事件可能很少见，并且在任何可行的计算预算下，仍然未被观察到。最近的保形生存框架构建了触发感兴趣事件所需迭代次数的可靠下限预测界（LPB），但依赖于静态预算分配，限制了多轮设置中的适应性。为了解决这个问题，我们引入了通过投影优化的动态分配（DAPRO），这是一个理论上有效的动态预算分配框架，用于界定多轮LLM交互中的事件发生时间。我们证明了DAPRO满足预期预算约束，并提供无分布、有限样本覆盖保证，而不需要先前保形生存方法假设的审查和事件时间之间的条件独立性。一个关键的理论贡献是一个新的覆盖界，它与仅在样本子集上计算的平均审查权重的平方根成比例，而不是最坏情况下的权重，从而提供比先前工作更紧凑的保证。此外，DAPRO可以在有限计算资源下获得人口级评估指标的无偏估计，例如越狱率。针对代理任务成功、LLM越狱、有毒内容生成和RAG幻觉的全面实验表明，DAPRO在不超过预期预算约束的情况下，达到了更接近名义水平的覆盖率，并且方差低于静态基线。
Summary / 总结
This article addresses the challenge of evaluating large language models (LLMs) in multi-turn conversational settings, where key events such as jailbreaks may only occur after numerous interactions, making traditional evaluation methods computationally expensive and often ineffective due to static budget allocation. Previous methods, particularly conformal survival frameworks, have limitations in adaptivity and rely on assumptions that may not hold in practice. The proposed Dynamic Allocation via PRojected Optimization (DAPRO) framework overcomes these issues by allowing for dynamic budget allocation, ensuring compliance with budget constraints while providing tighter coverage guarantees. The research methodology involves theoretical proofs of DAPRO's validity and practical applications to estimate population-level metrics like jailbreak rates. Experimental results indicate that DAPRO achieves better coverage and lower variance compared to static methods, effectively supporting the evaluation goals within the given computational budget.
本文解决了在多轮对话环境中评估大型语言模型（LLMs）的挑战，其中关键事件如越狱可能仅在多次交互后出现，这使得传统评估方法在计算上成本高且往往因静态预算分配而无效。以往的方法，特别是符合生存框架，存在适应性不足的局限，并依赖于在实践中可能不成立的假设。所提出的动态分配通过投影优化（DAPRO）框架克服了这些问题，允许动态预算分配，确保遵守预算约束，同时提供更紧密的覆盖保证。该方法论包括一种新颖的覆盖界限，通过关注样本的子集来改进现有方法，从而实现对评估指标（如越狱率）的无偏估计。实验结果表明，DAPRO在覆盖率和方差方面优于静态方法，有效支持其在有限计算资源下的目标。
FinRED: An Expert-Guided Benchmark Generation and Evaluation Framework for Financial LLM Red-Teaming
Authors: Chaeyun Kim, Daeyoung Park, Junghwan Kim, Jinyoung Jeong, Eunji Song, Yongtaek Lim, Minwoo Kim
First: 2026-06-18T07:46:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-22T05:37:13+00:00
Comments: Accepted at IEEE ICDM 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Existing safety benchmarks target general adversarial scenarios but miss finance-specific risks. Financial LLMs face regulatory compliance violations, fraud facilitation, and systemic trust erosion that require targeted evaluation. We introduce FinRED, an expert-guided red-teaming framework for financial LLM safety evaluation developed with financial experts. FinRED uses a novel two-level taxonomy mapping global standards (e.g., FATF and EU DORA) to threats ranging from regulatory evasion to complex fraud, integrated with a scalable pipeline that converts real financial documents into context-rich red-teaming Behavioral Prompts (seeds) through an expert-defined schema. Rigorous expert validation confirms seed plausibility and realism for meaningful LLM safety evaluation. We also provide an expert-validated, finance-specific rubric that goes beyond disclaimer checks, aligns more closely with human experts than static one-size-fits-all rubrics, and reduces critical false negatives from 28 to 12. Aligned with internationally adopted risk-management and information-security standards (e.g., ISO/IEC 27001), FinRED is deployed in South Korea's Financial Security Institute (FSI) regulatory sandbox for generative AI security evaluation in real financial services. To mitigate dual-use risks, the dataset, generation pipeline, prompt template, and evaluation framework are gated for qualified researchers at https://github.com/selectstar-ai/FinRED-paper and https://huggingface.co/datasets/datumo/FinRED.

Summary / 总结
This article addresses the gap in existing safety benchmarks for financial LLMs, which often overlook finance-specific risks such as regulatory compliance violations and fraud facilitation. Previous methods have primarily focused on general adversarial scenarios, leading to inadequate evaluations for the unique challenges in finance. The proposed FinRED framework, developed with input from financial experts, introduces a two-level taxonomy that maps global standards to specific threats and utilizes a scalable pipeline to create context-rich red-teaming Behavioral Prompts from real financial documents. This approach is well-motivated as it incorporates expert validation to ensure the realism and plausibility of the prompts, significantly reducing false negatives in safety evaluations. BreakFun: Jailbreaking LLMs via Object Instantiation under Simulated Code Execution
Authors: Amirkia Rafiei Oskooei, Mehmet S. Aktas
First: 2025-10-19T11:27:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-18T18:53:12+00:00
Comments: Accepted to AACL-IJCNLP 2026 Findings
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) are widely used because they process structures, syntax and code well, but this same ability also makes them paradoxically vulnerable. We introduce BreakFun, a jailbreak method that frames a harmful request as code-execution simulation. The prompt gives the model a benign Python class definition, the "Trojan Schema", and asks what that code would print if it ran. To answer, the model must create an object from the class and invent a value for each field, and the adversarial field names steer those values toward the attacker's goal. The harmful content therefore appears as a side-effect of the simulated instantiation, not as a direct answer. A three-part prompt carries the attack: an innocent frame, the Trojan Schema, and a Chain-of-Thought distraction. On JailbreakBench, BreakFun reaches an average attack success rate of 89% across 13 open-weight and commercial models (open-weight ~98%, API systems ~78%), and 100% on several. An ablation identifies the Trojan Schema as the strongest contributing component. As a counter-ablation probe we introduce Adversarial Prompt Deconstruction (APD), in which a second LLM writes out all readable text in the prompt before it judges safety. APD improves detection across three model families, and a probe-specific ablation shows that the transcription step gives most of the gain. Our findings show that LLM safety must account for content that a simulation task produces as a side-effect, not only for content that a user requests directly.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BreakFun：通过模拟代码执行中的对象实例化破解大型语言模型
大型语言模型（LLMs）因其良好的结构、语法和代码处理能力而被广泛使用，但这种能力也使其在某种程度上变得脆弱。我们介绍了BreakFun，这是一种将有害请求框架化为代码执行模拟的破解方法。提示给模型一个无害的Python类定义，即“特洛伊模式”，并询问如果运行该代码会打印什么。为了回答，模型必须从类中创建一个对象并为每个字段发明一个值，而对抗性字段名称则将这些值引导至攻击者的目标。因此，有害内容作为模拟实例化的副作用出现，而不是直接答案。攻击通过三部分提示进行：一个无辜的框架、特洛伊模式和一个思维链干扰。在JailbreakBench上，BreakFun在13个开放权重和商业模型中达到了89%的平均攻击成功率（开放权重约98%，API系统约78%），并在多个模型上达到了100%。消融实验确定特洛伊模式是最强的贡献成分。作为对抗消融探针，我们引入了对抗性提示解构（APD），其中第二个LLM在判断安全性之前写出提示中的所有可读文本。APD提高了三个模型家族的检测能力，而特定探针的消融显示转录步骤带来了大部分增益。我们的研究结果表明，LLM安全性必须考虑模拟任务作为副作用产生的内容，而不仅仅是用户直接请求的内容。

The proposed BreakFun method innovatively reframes harmful requests as code-execution simulations, utilizing a benign Python class definition to guide the model into generating harmful outputs as side effects rather than direct responses. This approach is well-motivated as it exploits the inherent capabilities of LLMs while circumventing traditional defenses. The paper contributes a novel jailbreak technique that achieves an average attack success rate of 89% across various models on the JailbreakBench, with some models reaching 100%. From Intent to Action: Benchmarking LLM Safety in Vehicle Voice Command Authorization
Authors: Diba Afroze, Xingli Zhang, Yazhou Tu, Xiali Hei
First: 2026-09-17T03:20:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-17T03:20:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into vehicle voice assistants. But linking natural-language requests to vehicle functions creates a safety-critical authorization problem. Before executing a command, the system must choose whether to execute, refuse, clarify, require confirmation, defer to manual control, trigger an emergency response, or make no tool call. To our knowledge, prior evaluations do not isolate this pre-action decision across speaker role, authentication status, vehicle state, and tool availability. We introduce a 202-scenario benchmark with Reference Decisions under a seven-class taxonomy. We evaluate two local open-weight models and three API-based LLMs using Decision Alignment and safety-specific error metrics. Alignment ranges from 40.1% for Llama 3.2 3B to 89.1% for Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview. The API-based models score between 83.2% and 89.1%, with no statistically significant differences among them. Even these models produce two to three False Executes among 161 non-execution scenarios, and persistent errors remain in confirmation and manual-control decisions. A controlled Llama 3.2 3B ablation increases alignment to 40.1% under the structured authorization policy, versus 28.2-29.2% under schema-only and generic-safety baselines, but it does not eliminate False Executes. Structured LLM decisions are therefore insufficient as a standalone safety mechanism, and deployment requires an independent enforcement layer that verifies tool permissions and vehicle-state constraints before invoking any vehicle function.
中文标题/摘要
